FILE 0004 / 0032
PATH: res://src/session_store.gd
ARCHIVE_PATH: DRENOVAR-iOS/project/src/session_store.gd
BYTES: 1588
SHA256: d8ce26d0c3c77eae633f6ce4e8dd52f814bf0cabcb67eb81fe80c2a2888cc1dd
ENDS_WITH_NEWLINE: true
````gdscript
extends RefCounted

const SESSION_PATH := "user://drenovar-session.bin"
const TOKEN_LENGTH := 129

static func valid(token: String) -> bool:
	if token.length() != TOKEN_LENGTH or token[64] != ".":
		return false
	for index in TOKEN_LENGTH:
		if index != 64 and not token[index] in "0123456789abcdef":
			return false
	return true

static func key() -> PackedByteArray:
	var device := OS.get_unique_id()
	if device.is_empty():
		return PackedByteArray()
	return ("DRENOVAR-iOS-session-v1:" + device).sha256_buffer()

static func read_session() -> String:
	var encryption_key := key()
	if encryption_key.is_empty() or not FileAccess.file_exists(SESSION_PATH):
		return ""
	var file := FileAccess.open_encrypted(SESSION_PATH, FileAccess.READ, encryption_key)
	if file == null or file.get_length() != TOKEN_LENGTH:
		return ""
	var token := file.get_as_text()
	return token if valid(token) else ""

static func save_session(token: String) -> Error:
	if not valid(token):
		return ERR_INVALID_DATA
	var encryption_key := key()
	if encryption_key.is_empty():
		return ERR_UNAVAILABLE
	var temporary := SESSION_PATH + ".tmp"
	var file := FileAccess.open_encrypted(temporary, FileAccess.WRITE, encryption_key)
	if file == null:
		return FileAccess.get_open_error()
	file.store_string(token)
	file.close()
	var result := DirAccess.rename_absolute(temporary, SESSION_PATH)
	if result != OK:
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(temporary)
	return result

static func forget() -> void:
	for path in [SESSION_PATH, SESSION_PATH + ".tmp"]:
		if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
			DirAccess.remove_absolute(path)
````
END FILE

FILE 0005 / 0032
PATH: res://src/battle_results.gd
ARCHIVE_PATH: DRENOVAR-iOS/project/src/battle_results.gd
BYTES: 1435
SHA256: 65cfb95605626376b3b153e7fd8317ff8e9989830f9263778d6154aba6fdc0b1
ENDS_WITH_NEWLINE: true
````gdscript
extends RefCounted

const PATH := "user://battle-results.cfg"
const ANNOUNCE_DELAY_MS := 1650

var storage := ConfigFile.new()
var profile := ""
var known := {}
var pending: Array[Dictionary] = []
var ready_at := 0

func _init() -> void:
	storage.load(PATH)

func observe(player: String, reports: Array) -> void:
	var first := 0
	if player != profile:
		reset()
		profile = player
		var last: String = storage.get_value("journal", profile, "")
		if last.is_empty():
			first = reports.size()
			if not reports.is_empty():
				remember(reports.back())
		else:
			var index := reports.find_custom(func(report): return report.get("id", "") == last)
			first = index + 1 if index >= 0 else reports.size()
	for index in reports.size():
		var report: Dictionary = reports[index]
		var id: String = report.get("id", "")
		if id.is_empty() or known.has(id):
			continue
		known[id] = true
		if index >= first:
			if pending.is_empty():
				ready_at = Time.get_ticks_msec() + ANNOUNCE_DELAY_MS
			pending.append(report)

func reset() -> void:
	profile = ""
	known.clear()
	pending.clear()
	ready_at = 0

func next() -> Dictionary:
	if pending.is_empty():
		return {}
	var report: Dictionary = pending.pop_front()
	remember(report)
	return report

func remember(report: Dictionary) -> void:
	var id: String = report.get("id", "")
	if not profile.is_empty() and not id.is_empty():
		storage.set_value("journal", profile, id)
		storage.save(PATH)
````
END FILE

````gdscript
extends SceneTree

func _initialize() -> void:
	call_deferred("run")

func run() -> void:
	var data: Dictionary = JSON.parse_string(FileAccess.get_file_as_string("res://assets/game.json"))
	var original = load("res://tests/reference052/landscape.gd")
	var candidate = load("res://tests/reference053/landscape.gd")
	var comparisons := 0
	var expected_upload := 0
	var candidate_upload := 0
	for scene in ["city","world"]:
		var before = original.new()
		var after = candidate.new()
		root.add_child(before)
		root.add_child(after)
		await before.setup(data,data.demo,scene,{})
		await after.setup(data,data.demo,scene,{})
		for i in 24:
			var previous_masks: Array = before.render_batches.map(func(b): return b.mask)
			var position := Vector3(sin(i*.3)*35,0,cos(i*.3)*25)
			var zoom: float = [32.0,70.0,110.0][i%3] if scene == "city" else [60.0,100.0,260.0][i%3]
			for stage in [before,after]:
				stage.target=position
				stage.zoom=zoom
				stage.world_yaw=0.5 if scene == "world" else 0.0
				stage.update_camera(0,true)
			if before.render_batches.size()!=after.render_batches.size():
				push_error("Batch count changed");quit(1);return
			for index in before.render_batches.size():
				var a: Dictionary=before.render_batches[index]
				var b: Dictionary=after.render_batches[index]
				comparisons+=1
				if a.node.visible!=b.node.visible or a.mask!=b.mask or a.visible!=b.visible or a.total!=b.total:
					push_error("Visible instances changed: "+scene);quit(1);return
				if a.visible > 0:
					var used:int=a.visible*12
					if a.node.multimesh.buffer.slice(0,used)!=b.node.multimesh.buffer.slice(0,used):
						push_error("Instance transform changed");quit(1);return
					if b.capacity>b.total or b.capacity<b.visible:
						push_error("Capacity bound violation");quit(1);return
					if previous_masks[index] != a.mask:
						expected_upload+=a.total*48
						candidate_upload+=b.capacity*48
		before.free()
		after.free()
		await process_frame
	print("QA_BATCH_PARITY comparisons=",comparisons," failures=0 reference_buffer_bytes=",expected_upload," candidate_buffer_bytes=",candidate_upload)
	quit()
````

````gdscript
extends SceneTree

const ReportFeed = preload("res://src/battle_results.gd")

var checks := 0
var failures := 0

func _initialize() -> void:
	call_deferred("run")

func check(condition: bool, detail: String) -> void:
	checks += 1
	if not condition:
		failures += 1
		push_error(detail)

func advance(game: Node, server_ms: int) -> void:
	var client := HTTPRequest.new()
	root.add_child(client)
	client.request(game.endpoint + "/test/clock?time=" + str(server_ms), [], HTTPClient.METHOD_POST)
	var response: Array = await client.request_completed
	client.queue_free()
	check(response[1] == 204, "Fixture clock update failed")

func has_label(parent: Node, text: String) -> bool:
	return parent.find_children("*", "Label", true, false).any(func(label): return label.text == text)

func check_battle(game: Node, city: bool) -> void:
	var target: Dictionary = (game.state.encounters if city else game.state.world.encounters)[0]
	var order := {"kind":"deploy" if city else "worldMarch", "encounterId":target.id,
		"units":{"ENG-03":int(game.state.healthy.get("ENG-03", 0))}}
	if not city:
		order.worldKind = "velkor"
	var start: Dictionary = await game.request("command", order)
	if not start.has("city"):
		check(false, str(start))
		return
	game.accept(start)
	var march: Dictionary = (start.armies if city else start.world.armies).back()
	var pre_food: int = int(game.balance("food"))
	var pre_xp: int = int(game.state.city.progress.xp)
	var item_id: String = target.spec.item
	var pre_items: int = int(game.state.items.get(item_id, 0))
	await advance(game, int(march.arrivesAt) + 10)
	var fighting: Dictionary = await game.request("state")
	var army: Dictionary = (fighting.armies if city else fighting.world.armies).back()
	await advance(game, int(army.battleEndsAt) + 1)
	var settled: Dictionary = await game.request("state")
	var report: Dictionary = settled.reports.back()
	check(report.outcome == "victory", "Fixture army must defeat " + target.spec.name)
	if report.outcome != "victory":
		return
	check(report.creature.species == target.spec.id, "Wrong reward report")
	check(int(settled.city.demo.wallet.food) - pre_food == int(report.rewards.resources.food), "Server food reward did not match the balance")
	check(int(settled.items[item_id]) - pre_items == int(report.rewards.items[item_id]), "Server material reward did not match inventory")
	check(int(settled.city.progress.xp) - pre_xp == int(report.rewards.xp), "Server XP did not match the report")
	if not city:
		game.open_modal("Проверка открытого окна")
	game.accept(settled)
	game.battle_results.ready_at = 0
	game.update_battle_result_notice()
	if not city:
		check(game.result_notice.visible and game.modal_title.text == "Проверка открытого окна", "Result interrupted an open panel or hid the notice")
		game.result_notice.pressed.emit()
	check(game.result_open and game.modal_title.text == "Победа", "Victory popup did not open")
	check(has_label(game.modal_body, "Еда +" + game.format_balance(int(report.rewards.resources.food))), "Popup did not show exact food")
	check(has_label(game.modal_body, "Опыт +" + game.format_balance(int(report.rewards.xp))), "Popup did not show exact XP")
	check(has_label(game.modal_body, ("Когти" if city else "Рога") + " +" + game.format_balance(int(report.rewards.items[item_id]))), "Popup did not show the trophy")
	game.close_modal()
	var unpaired_release := InputEventMouseButton.new()
	unpaired_release.button_index = MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT
	unpaired_release.position = game.stage.camera.unproject_position(game.stage.place_markers[0].position)
	game._unhandled_input(unpaired_release)
	check(not game.modal.visible, "Closing the result selected a map marker")
	var reopened := ReportFeed.new()
	reopened.observe(game.player_id, settled.reports)
	check(reopened.pending.is_empty(), "Read result reappeared after reopening the game")
	game.accept(settled)
	game.update_battle_result_notice()
	check(not game.modal.visible, "Same result reappeared")
	await advance(game, int(army.battleEndsAt) + int(march.travelMs) + 1000)
	game.accept(await game.request("state"))

func run() -> void:
	root.size = Vector2i(1366, 630)
	var game = load("res://main.tscn").instantiate()
	root.add_child(game)
	while not is_instance_valid(game.stage) or game.scene_loading:
		await process_frame
	game.set_process(false)
	await game.show_scene("world")
	await check_battle(game, false)
	await game.show_scene("city")
	await check_battle(game, true)
	print("QA_BATTLE_REWARDS checks=", checks, " failures=", failures)
	quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
````

````gdscript
			var b: Button = game.resource_buttons[key]
			var rect := b.get_global_rect()
			check(rect.position.x >= previous_end and rect.end.x <= logical_size.x - 40, "HUD resource overlap or clipping: " + key)
			check(rect.size.y <= 44.1, "Resource strip unexpectedly tall")
			check(b.get_theme_stylebox("normal").bg_color.a == 0, "Resource card background remains")
			previous_end = rect.end.x
		for scene in ["city", "world"]:
			if scene != game.map_name:
				await game.show_scene(scene)

			for view_zoom in ([32.0, 70.0, 110.0] if scene == "city" else [45.0, 100.0, 260.0]):
				stage.target = Vector3.ZERO
				stage.zoom = view_zoom
				stage.world_yaw = 0 if scene == "city" else 0.7
				stage.update_camera(0, true)
				stage.begin_direct_manipulation()
				var origin := logical_size * Vector2(0.45, 0.48)
				var destination := origin + Vector2(37, 19)
				var anchor: Vector3 = stage.ground_at(origin)
				stage.drag_between(origin, destination)
				print("ANCHOR ", scene, " z=", view_zoom, " error=", stage.camera.unproject_position(anchor).distance_to(destination))
				check(stage.camera.unproject_position(anchor).distance_to(destination) < 0.1, "Drag anchor drift: " + scene)
				check(stage.target.is_equal_approx(stage.camera_target), "Camera still follows with lag")
				var before: Vector3 = stage.camera.position
				stage.update_camera(1.0 / 60.0)
				check(stage.camera.position.is_equal_approx(before), "Camera continues chasing after finger stops")
				anchor = stage.ground_at(destination)
				stage.pinch_between(destination, destination + Vector2(11, -7), 1.2)
				check(stage.camera.unproject_position(anchor).distance_to(destination + Vector2(11, -7)) < 0.1, "Pinch anchor drift: " + scene)
				check(is_equal_approx(stage.camera_zoom, stage.zoom), "Pinch still interpolates")
			# Scripted centering remains smooth; taking over does not jump to its target.
			stage.target += Vector3(12, 0, 6)
			stage.update_camera(1.0 / 60.0)
			check(stage.target.distance_to(stage.camera_target) > 0.1, "Scripted fly-to lost smoothing")
			var displayed: Vector3 = stage.camera_target
			stage.begin_direct_manipulation()
			check(stage.target == displayed, "Touch interrupts fly-to with a jump")
	# Exercise real touch handlers, including canceled release and pinch-to-one-finger transition.
	await game.show_scene("city")
	var touch := InputEventScreenTouch.new()
	touch.index = 0
	touch.position = Vector2(500, 280)
	touch.pressed = true
	game._unhandled_input(touch)
	var drag := InputEventScreenDrag.new()
	drag.index = 0
	drag.position = Vector2(530, 290)
	drag.relative = Vector2(30, 10)
	game._input(drag)
	check(game.stage.camera_target == game.stage.target, "Real handler delays pan")

	game._unhandled_input(touch)
	drag.index = 1
	drag.position = Vector2(740, 280)
	drag.relative = Vector2(40, 0)
	game._input(drag)
	touch.pressed = false
	game._input(touch)
	drag.index = 0
	drag.position = Vector2(540, 290)
	drag.relative = Vector2(10, 0)
	game._input(drag)
	check(game.fingers.size() == 1, "Pinch release loses surviving finger")
	touch.index = 0
	touch.canceled = true
	game._input(touch)
	check(game.fingers.is_empty() and not game.tap_candidate, "Canceled gesture sticks")
	game.show_economy("food")
	var saved_target: Vector3 = game.stage.target
	game._input(drag)
	check(game.stage.target == saved_target, "Modal allows camera gesture")
	game.close_modal()
	var saved_food: int = game.balance("food")
	game.state.city.demo.wallet.food = game.capacity() + 1500
	game.show_economy("food")
	check(game.modal_body.find_children("*", "Label", true, false).any(func(label): return label.text.begins_with("Производство приостановлено")), "Over-cap message missing")
	game.close_modal()

	check(not game.modal_body.find_children("*", "Label", true, false).any(func(label): return label.text.begins_with("Производство приостановлено")), "Under-cap message incorrect")
	game.state.city.demo.wallet.food = saved_food
	print("QA_CAMERA_HUD checks=", checks, " failures=", failures.size())
	quit(0 if failures.is_empty() else 1)
````

````gdscript
extends SceneTree

const Store = preload("res://src/session_store.gd")
var failures := 0
var checks := 0

func _initialize() -> void:
	call_deferred("run")

func check(value: bool, description: String) -> void:
	checks += 1
	if not value:
		failures += 1
		push_error(description)

func touch(index: int, at: Vector2, pressed: bool) -> void:
	var event := InputEventScreenTouch.new()
	event.index = index
	event.position = at
	event.pressed = pressed
	root.push_input(event, true)

func check_panel(game: Node) -> void:
	var emulation := Input.emulate_mouse_from_touch
	game.open_modal("Прокрутка")
	var taps := [0]
	var first: Button
	for i in 25:
		var entry: Button = game.button(game.modal_body, "Строка %d" % (i + 1), func(): taps[0] += 1)
		if i == 0:
			first = entry
	await process_frame
	await process_frame
	var scroll: ScrollContainer = game.modal_scroll
	check(not Input.emulate_mouse_from_touch, "Modal must own its touch gestures")
	check(scroll.get_v_scroll_bar().max_value > scroll.get_v_scroll_bar().page, "Test panel must overflow")
	var start := first.get_global_rect().get_center()
	var end := start - Vector2(0, 170)
	var previous_target: Vector3 = game.stage.target
	touch(0, start, true)
	var drag := InputEventScreenDrag.new()
	drag.index = 0
	drag.position = end
	drag.relative = end - start
	root.push_input(drag, true)
	touch(0, end, false)
	await process_frame
	check(scroll.scroll_vertical >= 160, "Dragging on a button did not scroll the panel")
	check(taps[0] == 0, "Scrolling activated a button")
	check(game.stage.target == previous_target, "Panel gesture moved the camera")
	start = scroll.get_global_rect().get_center()
	end = start + Vector2(0, 180)
	touch(0, start, true)
	drag.position = end
	drag.relative = end - start
	root.push_input(drag, true)
	touch(0, end, false)
	await process_frame
	check(scroll.scroll_vertical == 0, "Reverse finger drag did not scroll back to the start")
	start = first.get_global_rect().get_center()
	touch(0, start, true)
	touch(0, start, false)
	await process_frame
	check(taps[0] == 1, "Finger tap did not activate the button")
	game.close_modal()
	check(Input.emulate_mouse_from_touch == emulation, "Closing the panel changed touch settings")

func run() -> void:
	root.size = Vector2i(1366, 630)
	var scenario := ""
	for arg in OS.get_cmdline_user_args():
		if arg.begins_with("--case="):
			scenario = arg.trim_prefix("--case=")
	var game = load("res://main.tscn").instantiate()
	game.endpoint = "http://127.0.0.1:8426"
	root.add_child(game)
	# The native splash is present before game assets and account data load.
	check(game.get_children().any(func(node): return node is CanvasLayer and node.layer == 10), "Launch layer is missing")
	while game.scene_loading or not is_instance_valid(game.stage):
		await process_frame
	await process_frame
	game.set_process(false)
	check(not game.get_children().any(func(node): return node is CanvasLayer and node.layer == 10), "Launch layer blocked a ready city")
	match scenario:
		"login":
			check(Store.read_session().is_empty(), "Login case started with a persisted session")
			game.login_proof = "a".repeat(64)
			game.login_deadline = Time.get_ticks_msec() + 5000
			await game.finish_login(true)
			check(game.player_id == "native-qa-player", "Native sign-in did not load the settlement")
			check(Store.valid(Store.read_session()), "Successful login did not persist the token")
			check(FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes(Store.SESSION_PATH).size() > 0, "Encrypted session file missing")
			var stored_bytes := FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes(Store.SESSION_PATH)
			check(not stored_bytes.hex_encode().contains(game.session.to_utf8_buffer().hex_encode()), "Raw token is visible in the stored file")
			await check_panel(game)
		"restore":
			check(game.player_id == "native-qa-player" and not game.restoring_session, "Cold launch did not restore account")
			check(Store.valid(Store.read_session()), "Restored token disappeared")
		"offline":
			check(game.restoring_session and game.player_id.is_empty(), "Temporary failure should leave restoration pending")
			check(Store.valid(Store.read_session()), "Temporary failure erased login")
		"expired":
			check(game.player_id.is_empty() and game.session.is_empty(), "Expired session remained connected")
			check(Store.read_session().is_empty(), "Expired session was not removed")
		"logout":
			check(game.player_id == "native-qa-player", "Logout case did not restore account")
			await game.sign_out()
			check(game.session.is_empty() and Store.read_session().is_empty(), "Sign out did not clear the saved session")
		_:
			check(false, "Expected a QA scenario")
	print("QA_IOS_STARTUP ", scenario, " checks=", checks, " failures=", failures)
	quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
````

PATH: res://qa_optimization.gd
ARCHIVE_PATH: DRENOVAR-iOS/project/qa_optimization.gd

SHA256: 14c74ca675249c6ad640f875fc578c2416f7a1164bf72291987aea115c7d6a0e
ENDS_WITH_NEWLINE: true
````gdscript
extends SceneTree

func _initialize() -> void:
	call_deferred("run")

func submitted(stage: Node) -> Vector2i:
	var counts := Vector2i.ZERO
	for batch in stage.render_batches:
		if not batch.node.visible:
			continue
		var mesh: Mesh = batch.node.multimesh.mesh
		var triangles := 0
		for surface in mesh.get_surface_count():
			var indices: int = mesh.surface_get_array_index_len(surface) if mesh is ArrayMesh else 0
			var vertices: int = mesh.surface_get_array_len(surface) if mesh is ArrayMesh else 6
			triangles += int((indices if indices > 0 else vertices) / 3.0)
		counts += Vector2i(batch.visible, triangles * batch.visible)
	return counts

func check_batches(before: Node, after: Node) -> bool:
	for index in before.render_batches.size():
		var old: Dictionary = before.render_batches[index]
		var candidate: Dictionary = after.render_batches[index]
		if old.total != candidate.total or old.layer != candidate.layer:
			return false
		if old.node.visible != candidate.node.visible:
			return false
		if not old.node.visible:
			continue
		if old.mask != candidate.mask or old.visible != candidate.visible:
			return false
		var used: int = old.visible * 12
		if old.node.multimesh.buffer.slice(0, used) != candidate.node.multimesh.buffer.slice(0, used):
			return false
	return true

func run() -> void:
	root.size = Vector2i(1366, 630)
	var data: Dictionary = JSON.parse_string(FileAccess.get_file_as_string("res://assets/game.json"))
	var old_scene = load("res://tests/reference053/landscape.gd")
	var new_scene = load("res://src/landscape.gd")
	var comparisons := 0
	for scene in ["city", "world"]:
		var before = old_scene.new()
		var after = new_scene.new()
		root.add_child(before)
		root.add_child(after)
		await before.setup(data, data.demo, scene, {})
		await after.setup(data, data.demo, scene, {})
		for zoom: float in ([32.0, 70.0, 110.0] if scene == "city" else [60.0, 100.0, 260.0]):
			var counts := [Vector2i.ZERO, Vector2i.ZERO]
			var times := [[], []]
			for i in 20:
				for j in 2:
					var stage = [before, after][j]
					stage.target = Vector3(5 + sin(i * 0.25) * 30, 0, -4 + cos(i * 0.25) * 18)
					stage.zoom = zoom
					stage.world_yaw = 0.5 if scene == "world" else 0.0
					var start := Time.get_ticks_usec()
					stage.update_camera(0, true)
					times[j].append((Time.get_ticks_usec() - start) / 1000.0)
					counts[j] += submitted(stage)
				if before.render_batches.size() != after.render_batches.size() or not check_batches(before, after):
					push_error("Placement, LOD or visibility changed in %s at %.0f" % [scene, zoom])
					quit(1)
					return
				comparisons += after.render_batches.size()
			for samples in times:
				samples.sort()
			print(JSON.stringify({"scene": scene, "zoom": zoom, "instances_before": counts[0].x, "instances_after": counts[1].x, "triangles_before": counts[0].y, "triangles_after": counts[1].y, "cull_before_ms": times[0][10], "cull_after_ms": times[1][10]}))
		before.free()
		after.free()
	print("QA_OPTIMIZATION comparisons=", comparisons, " failures=0")
	quit()
````

````gdscript
func run() -> void:
	var game = load("res://main.tscn").instantiate()
	root.add_child(game)
	while game.scene_loading or not is_instance_valid(game.stage):
		await process_frame
	var result: Array = []
	var tag := "native-profile"
	for arg in OS.get_cmdline_user_args():
		if arg.begins_with("--tag="):
			tag = arg.trim_prefix("--tag=").validate_filename()
	for spec in [["city",70.0], ["city",32.0], ["world",100.0]]:

		result.append(row)
		print("RENDER_PROFILE ", JSON.stringify(row))
		FileAccess.open("user://"+tag+"-render.json",FileAccess.WRITE).store_string(JSON.stringify(result,"  "))
	await game.show_scene("city")
	print("RENDER_PROFILE_DONE ", tag)

	quit()
````

````gdscript
	call_deferred("run")

func run() -> void:
	var game = load("res://main.tscn").instantiate()
	root.add_child(game)
	while not is_instance_valid(game.stage) or game.scene_loading:
		await process_frame

	check(not local_state.encounters.is_empty(), "Fixture needs creatures")
	var encounter: Dictionary = local_state.encounters[0]

		stage.present_creatures(local_state,float(local_state.serverTime),.016)

	check(creature.process_mode == Node.PROCESS_MODE_DISABLED,"Hidden creature still animates")
	stage.camera_target = stage.target
	stage.present_creatures(local_state,float(local_state.serverTime)+32,.016)
	check(creature.visible and creature.get_instance_id() == identity,"Re-entry rebuilt creature")
	encounter.status = "defeated"
	stage.present_creatures(local_state,float(local_state.serverTime)+48,.016)
	await process_frame
	check(not is_instance_valid(creature),"Defeated creature kept in cache")
	var army := {"id":"qa_runtime_march","status":"outbound","units":{"ENG-01":4},"path":[{"x":0,"z":0},{"x":10,"z":0}],"startsAt":0,"arrivesAt":10000}

	stage.target = Vector3.ZERO
	stage.zoom = 40
	stage.update_camera(0,true)
	stage.present_armies(local_state,5000)
	var group = stage.actors.get_node_or_null("qa_runtime_march")
	check(group != null and group.get_child_count() == 4,"March composition changed")
	var unit_id: int = group.get_child(0).get_instance_id()
	stage.target = Vector3(140,0,110)
	stage.update_camera(0,true)
	stage.present_armies(local_state,5016)
	check(not group.visible and not group.is_queued_for_deletion(),"Camera exit destroyed army")
	stage.target = Vector3.ZERO
	stage.update_camera(0,true)
	stage.present_armies(local_state,5032)
	check(group.visible and group.get_child(0).get_instance_id() == unit_id,"Re-entry rebuilt army")
	army.status = "returned"
	stage.present_armies(local_state,5048)
	await process_frame
	check(not is_instance_valid(group),"Returned army remained alive")

	var parent := Node3D.new()
	stage.add_child(parent)
	var known := {}
	for i in 12:
		var actor := Node3D.new()
		actor.name = "cached"+str(i)
		actor.set_meta("last_visible_ticks",Time.get_ticks_msec())
		parent.add_child(actor)
		known[String(actor.name)] = true
	stage.retain_hidden_actors(parent,known,{},8)
	await process_frame
	check(parent.get_child_count() == 8,"Hidden cache exceeds cap")
	for child in parent.get_children():
		child.set_meta("last_visible_ticks",Time.get_ticks_msec()-11000)
	stage.retain_hidden_actors(parent,known,{},8)
	await process_frame
	check(parent.get_child_count() == 0,"Expired hidden cache retained")
	await game.show_scene("world")
	check(game.nav_buttons.city.visible and not game.nav_buttons.world.visible,"World has one city switch")
	var city = game.scene_cache.city
	game.smoke = true
	game.scene_switch_msec -= 5000
	game._process(1.0 / 60.0)
	check(game.scene_cache.get("city") == city, "City cache expired after a brief visit to the world")
	await game.show_scene("city")
	check(game.stage == city, "Returning to city rebuilt a cached scene")
	await game.show_scene("world")
	game.release_inactive_scene()
	check(is_instance_valid(city) and game.retired_nodes.size() > 48,"Scene was destroyed in one burst")
	var pending: int = game.retired_nodes.size()
	game.drain_retired_scenes()
	check(pending-game.retired_nodes.size() <= 48,"Disposal node budget exceeded")
	while not game.retired_nodes.is_empty():
		game.drain_retired_scenes()
	check(not is_instance_valid(city) and is_instance_valid(game.stage.camera),"Disposal freed active scene or leaked old scene")
	print("QA_RUNTIME checks=",checks," failures=",failures.size())
	quit(0 if failures.is_empty() else 1)
````

````gdscript
	call_deferred("run")

func check(condition: bool, detail: String) -> void:
	checks += 1
	if not condition:
		failures += 1
		push_error(detail)

func run() -> void:
	var data: Dictionary = JSON.parse_string(FileAccess.get_file_as_string("res://assets/game.json"))
	check(data.unitModels.size() == 10, "All ten warrior definitions are required")
	for id in data.unitModels:
		var visual: Dictionary = data.unitModels[id]
		for role in ["preview", "field"]:
			var packed := load(visual[role]) as PackedScene
			check(packed != null, "%s %s model missing" % [id, role])
			if packed == null:
				continue
			var actor := packed.instantiate() as Node3D
			Warriors.prepare(actor)
			var materials := {}
			for part: MeshInstance3D in actor.find_children("*", "MeshInstance3D", true, false):
				for surface in part.mesh.get_surface_count():
					var material := part.get_active_material(surface) as BaseMaterial3D
					if material != null:
						materials[material.get_instance_id()] = material
			check(not materials.is_empty(), "%s %s has no surfaces" % [id, role])
			check(materials.values().all(func(m): return m.has_meta("drenovar_warrior_surface")),
				"%s %s has untreated surfaces" % [id, role])
			var textured: int = materials.values().filter(func(m): return m.albedo_texture != null and m.normal_enabled).size()
			check(textured > 0, "%s %s has no textured PBR surfaces" % [id, role])
			var clips := PackedStringArray()
			for player: AnimationPlayer in actor.find_children("*", "AnimationPlayer", true, false):
				clips.append_array(player.get_animation_list())
			for required in ["Idle", "Walk", visual.drill]:
				check(clips.has(required), "%s %s misses %s" % [id, role, required])
			if role == "preview" and id in ["ENG-05", "ENG-06"]:
				check(clips.has("Recruit_Showcase"), id + " lacks mounted showcase")
			actor.free()
			print(id, " ", role, " · ", materials.size(), " materials, ", textured, " with albedo and normal maps")
			await process_frame
	print("QA_WARRIORS checks=", checks, " failures=", failures)
	quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
````

````gdscript
extends SceneTree

var checks := 0
var failures := 0

func _initialize() -> void:
	call_deferred("run")

func check(condition: bool, description: String) -> void:
	checks += 1
	if not condition:
		failures += 1
		push_error(description)

func has_label(game: Node, expected: String) -> bool:
	return game.modal_body.find_children("*", "Label", true, false).any(
		func(label): return label.text == expected
	)

func run() -> void:
	root.size = Vector2i(1366, 630)
	var game = load("res://main.tscn").instantiate()
	root.add_child(game)
	while not is_instance_valid(game.stage) or game.scene_loading:
		await process_frame
	game.set_process(false)
	if game.player_id.is_empty():
		push_error("Start the seeded web v91 QA server and pass --server=http://127.0.0.1:4187")
		quit(1)
		return
	var start_food: int = game.balance("food")
	check(start_food >= 2400 and start_food < 2420 and game.capacity() == 2800, "Seed balance or capacity differs")
	game.show_economy("food")
	check(has_label(game, "+120/мин · предел производства — 2 800"), "Farm should be producing below the limit")
	await create_timer(1.25).timeout
	var updated: Dictionary = await game.request("state")
	game.accept(updated)
	check(game.balance("food") > start_food, "Server farm production did not increase food")
	check(has_label(game, "Еда — " + game.format_balance(game.balance("food"))), "Open economy panel did not update")
	game.show_starter_goals()
	check(has_label(game, "Первые шаги · 2/5"), "Initial starter progress differs")
	var claim := game.modal_body.find_children("*", "Button", false, false).filter(
		func(button): return button.text == "Получить награду"
	).front() as Button
	check(claim != null and not claim.disabled, "Training reward should be available")
	if claim == null or claim.disabled:
		quit(1)
		return
	var before: int = game.balance("food")
	claim.pressed.emit()
	var deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + 6000
	while game.command_in_progress and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:
		await process_frame
	check(game.balance("food") - before >= 60 and game.balance("food") - before <= 62, "Server did not credit the 60-food reward")
	check(game.modal.visible and has_label(game, "Первые шаги · 3/5"), "Claim did not advance the open panel")
	var refreshed: Dictionary = await game.request("state")
	check(refreshed.city.kingdom.guide.starterClaims71.has("train"), "Claim was missing after reloading state")
	var excessive := refreshed.duplicate(true)
	excessive.city.demo.wallet.food = 4300
	game.show_economy("food")
	game.accept(excessive)
	check(has_label(game, "Еда — 4 300"), "Over-cap food balance was clipped or stale")
	check(has_label(game, "Производство приостановлено · предел производства — 2 800"), "Over-cap production status differs")
	game.show_report({"creature":{"name":"Тенерис I"}, "outcome":"victory", "rewards":{"resources":{"food":1500}}})
	check(has_label(game, "Еда +1 500"), "Battle report did not display the credited amount")
	game.accept(refreshed)
	await game.show_scene("world")
	game.stage.world_yaw = 0.6
	game.stage.zoom = 37
	game.stage.target = Vector3(70, 0, 25)
	game.show_world_overview()
	game.stage.update_camera(0, true)
	check(is_zero_approx(game.stage.world_yaw), "Full world view did not reset rotation")
	for i in 8:
		var point: Vector3 = game.stage.terrain_bounds.get_endpoint(i)
		check(root.get_visible_rect().has_point(game.stage.camera.unproject_position(point)), "Map bounds were clipped")
	var creature: Dictionary = game.state.world.encounters[0].duplicate(true)
	creature.status = "defeated"
	creature.hp = 0
	var target: Vector3 = game.stage.target
	var zoom: float = game.stage.zoom
	game.inspect_encounter(creature)
	check(game.stage.target == target and game.stage.zoom == zoom, "Defeated creature moved the camera")
	game.show_relocation()
	check(game.relocation_pins.visible and not game.modal.visible, "Relocation mode did not open on map")
	var site: Dictionary = game.stage.empty_plots[0]
	game.pick_marker(game.stage.camera.unproject_position(site.position))
	check(game.modal.visible and game.modal_title.text == "Свободное место", "Vacant location did not open confirmation")
	game.close_modal()
	check(not game.relocation_pins.visible and not game.modal.visible, "Close did not dismiss relocation")
	print("QA_WEB91 checks=", checks, " failures=", failures)
	quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
````

````gdscript
extends SceneTree

var checks := 0
var failures := 0
var capture_dir := ""

func _initialize() -> void:
	call_deferred("run")

func check(condition: bool, description: String) -> void:
	checks += 1
	if not condition:
		failures += 1
		push_error(description)

func find_button(parent: Node, title: String) -> Button:
	for button: Button in parent.find_children("*", "Button", true, false):
		if button.text == title:
			return button
	return null

func settle_and_capture(name: String) -> void:
	await process_frame
	await process_frame
	if not capture_dir.is_empty():
		await create_timer(0.34).timeout
		await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
		root.get_texture().get_image().save_png(capture_dir.path_join(name + ".png"))

func check_safe(game: Node) -> void:
	var bounds := Rect2(Vector2.ZERO, root.get_visible_rect().size)
	check(bounds.encloses(game.modal.get_global_rect()), "Panel went outside the viewport: " + game.modal_title.text)
	check(game.modal.get_global_rect().encloses(game.modal_scroll.get_global_rect()), "Scroll escaped panel")

func run() -> void:
	for argument in OS.get_cmdline_user_args():
		if argument.begins_with("--capture="):
			capture_dir = argument.trim_prefix("--capture=")
	root.size = Vector2i(1366, 630)
	var game = load("res://main.tscn").instantiate()
	root.add_child(game)
	while not is_instance_valid(game.stage) or game.scene_loading:
		await process_frame
	game.set_process(false)
	if game.player_id.is_empty():
		game.player_id = "fixture"
		game.state = game.state.duplicate(true)
		game.state.healthy = {"ENG-09": 18, "ENG-01": 6}
	var barracks: Dictionary = game.recruitment_buildings()[0]
	game.inspect_building(barracks)
	var recruit := find_button(game.modal_body, "Подготовка войск")
	check(recruit != null, "Recruitment could not be reached from the military building")
	recruit.pressed.emit()
	var immediate_preview := game.modal_body.find_child("RecruitPreview", true, false) as SubViewportContainer
	check(immediate_preview != null and immediate_preview.get_child_count() == 0, "Recruitment waited for the live model")
	check(immediate_preview.get_parent().get_node("PreviewFallback").texture != null, "Recruitment has no immediate portrait")
	await settle_and_capture("recruitment")
	check(game.modal_title.text.begins_with("Казармы пехоты · ур."), "Recruitment heading differs")
	check(game.modal_body.find_child("RecruitPreview", true, false) != null, "Missing 3D preview")
	check(find_button(game.modal_body, "Пеший новобранец") != null, "Missing roster card")
	check(find_button(game.modal_footer, "Готовые войска →") != null, "Missing garrison link")
	check_safe(game)
	var slider := game.modal_body.find_children("*", "HSlider", true, false)[0] as HSlider
	var at := slider.get_global_rect().get_center()
	var touch := InputEventScreenTouch.new()
	touch.pressed = true
	touch.position = at
	root.push_input(touch, true)
	var drag := InputEventScreenDrag.new()
	drag.position = at + Vector2(50, 1)
	drag.relative = Vector2(50, 1)
	root.push_input(drag, true)
	touch.pressed = false
	touch.position = drag.position
	root.push_input(touch, true)
	check(slider.value > 50, "Horizontal finger movement did not update the slider")
	check(game.modal_scroll.scroll_vertical == 0, "Slider swipe also scrolled the panel")
	find_button(game.modal_body, "10").pressed.emit()
	check(slider.value == 10, "Quantity shortcut did not update the slider")
	var selected := find_button(game.modal_body, "Копейщик")
	check(selected != null and selected.icon != null, "Missing original spearman portrait")
	selected.pressed.emit()
	await process_frame
	check(game.modal_body.find_children("*", "Label", true, false).any(func(label): return label.text == "Копейщик"), "Unit switch did not update the preview caption")
	if game.loopback:
		game.open_recruitment(barracks, "ENG-09", 2)
		var train := game.modal_body.find_children("*", "Button", true, false).filter(func(button): return button.text.begins_with("Обучить 2"))[0] as Button
		check(not train.disabled, "Training submission unexpectedly disabled")
		var wood := int(game.balance("wood"))
		var food := int(game.balance("food"))
		var coins := int(game.balance("coins"))
		train.pressed.emit()
		check(train.disabled and train.text == "Создаём заказ…", "Train button did not acknowledge the tap immediately")
		check(game.balance("wood") == wood, "The UI deducted resources before server confirmation")
		while game.command_in_progress:
			await process_frame
		var queue: Array = game.state.city.military.queues.get(barracks.id, [])
		check(queue.size() == 1 and queue[0].quantity == 2, "Recruitment action did not submit the chosen quantity")
		var charged: Dictionary = queue[0].get("chargedCost", {}) if not queue.is_empty() else {}
		check(int(charged.get("wood", -1)) == 50 and int(charged.get("food", -1)) == 60
			and int(charged.get("iron", -1)) == 0 and int(charged.get("coins", -1)) == 40,
			"Confirmed training did not record the expected cost: " + str(charged))
		check(game.balance("wood") < wood and game.balance("food") < food and game.balance("coins") < coins,
			"Confirmed training did not lower the displayed balances")
		check(game.modal_body.find_children("*", "Label", true, false).any(func(label): return label.text == "ОЧЕРЕДЬ 1 / 3"), "Queue was not refreshed after recruitment")
	game.close_modal()
	check(not game.modal.visible and game.modal_body.get_child_count() == 0, "Close did not clear the preview")
	var target: Dictionary = game.state.encounters[0]
	game.inspect_encounter(target)
	await settle_and_capture("city-target")
	check(game.modal.anchor_left == 0 and game.modal.anchor_right < 0.5, "City target is not docked on the left")
	check(find_button(game.modal_footer, "Атаковать") != null, "City attack action missing")
	check_safe(game)
	find_button(game.modal_footer, "Атаковать").pressed.emit()
	await settle_and_capture("city-march")
	check(game.modal_title.text == "Создать войско", "City order opened the wrong window")
	check(find_button(game.modal_body, "Все доступные") != null, "Missing select all action")
	check(find_button(game.modal_footer, "Марш") != null, "Missing march action")
	for amount: SpinBox in game.modal_body.find_children("*", "SpinBox", true, false):
		check(amount.get_line_edit().text.to_int() == int(amount.value), "Bulk selection left the displayed quantity out of date")
	check_safe(game)
	game.show_economy("food")
	await settle_and_capture("resources")
	check(game.modal.anchor_left == 0, "Resources did not dock on the left")
	check_safe(game)
	await game.show_scene("world")
	game.inspect_encounter(game.state.world.encounters[0])
	await settle_and_capture("world-target")
	check(game.modal.anchor_left > 0.5, "World target is not docked on the right")
	check(find_button(game.modal_footer, "Собрать отряд") != null, "Missing assemble action")
	check_safe(game)
	find_button(game.modal_footer, "Собрать отряд").pressed.emit()
	await settle_and_capture("world-march")
	check(game.modal_title.text == "Сбор отряда", "World march opened the wrong window")
	check(game.modal_footer.find_child("MarchSend", true, false).disabled, "Empty party can be sent")
	check_safe(game)
	if game.loopback:
		var quantity := game.modal_body.find_children("*", "SpinBox", true, false)[0] as SpinBox
		quantity.value = 1
		var send := game.modal_footer.find_child("MarchSend", true, false) as Button
		var deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + 6500
		while send.disabled and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:
			await process_frame
		check(not send.disabled, "Valid world party did not get a server route")
		send.pressed.emit()
		while game.command_in_progress:
			await process_frame
		check(game.state.world.armies.size() == 1, "World send button did not create an army")
		check(game.modal_title.text == "Общий мир", "World march did not return to the directory")
	print("QA_WEB_PANELS checks=", checks, " failures=", failures)
	quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
````

````glsl
shader_type spatial;

uniform sampler2D grass : source_color, filter_linear_mipmap_anisotropic, repeat_enable;
uniform sampler2D earth : source_color, filter_linear_mipmap_anisotropic, repeat_enable;
uniform sampler2D rock : source_color, filter_linear_mipmap_anisotropic, repeat_enable;
uniform sampler2D normal_texture : hint_normal, filter_linear_mipmap_anisotropic, repeat_enable;
uniform sampler2D roughness_texture : filter_linear_mipmap_anisotropic, repeat_enable;
uniform sampler2D soil_normal : hint_normal, filter_linear_mipmap_anisotropic, repeat_enable;
uniform sampler2D soil_roughness : filter_linear_mipmap_anisotropic, repeat_enable;
uniform sampler2D stone_normal : hint_normal, filter_linear_mipmap_anisotropic, repeat_enable;
uniform sampler2D stone_roughness : filter_linear_mipmap_anisotropic, repeat_enable;
uniform bool settlement = false;
varying vec3 terrain_position;
varying vec3 terrain_normal;

void vertex() {
	terrain_position = VERTEX;
	terrain_normal = NORMAL;
}

void fragment() {
	vec3 land = texture(grass, UV).rgb;
	if (settlement) {
		land = mix(land, texture(earth, UV).rgb, UV2.x);
		land = mix(land, texture(rock, UV).rgb, UV2.y);
	} else {
		vec3 axis = pow(abs(normalize(terrain_normal)), vec3(4.0));
		axis /= max(0.001, axis.x + axis.y + axis.z);
		vec3 stone = texture(rock, terrain_position.yz / 5.0).rgb * axis.x
			+ texture(rock, terrain_position.xz / 5.0).rgb * axis.y
			+ texture(rock, terrain_position.xy / 5.0).rgb * axis.z;
		vec3 tint = vec3(0.96 + 0.045 * sin(terrain_position.x * 0.19 + sin(terrain_position.z * 0.11)), 0.985, 0.96);
		land = mix(land * tint, texture(earth, UV * 0.85).rgb, UV2.x * 0.8);
		land = mix(land, stone, UV2.y);
		land = mix(land, vec3(0.79, 0.82, 0.8), clamp((terrain_position.y - 25.0) / 4.0, 0.0, 1.0));
	}
	ALBEDO = land * COLOR.rgb;
	NORMAL_MAP = texture(normal_texture, UV).rgb;
	NORMAL_MAP_DEPTH = settlement ? 0.51 : 0.24;
	ROUGHNESS = texture(roughness_texture, UV).r;
	if (settlement) {
		vec3 detail = mix(NORMAL_MAP * 2.0 - 1.0, texture(soil_normal, UV).rgb * 2.0 - 1.0, UV2.x);
		detail = mix(detail, texture(stone_normal, UV).rgb * 2.0 - 1.0, UV2.y);
		NORMAL_MAP = normalize(detail) * 0.5 + 0.5;
		ROUGHNESS = mix(ROUGHNESS, texture(soil_roughness, UV).r, UV2.x);
		ROUGHNESS = mix(ROUGHNESS, texture(stone_roughness, UV).r, UV2.y);
	}
}
````

````gdscript
extends RefCounted

const GROUPS := ["INF-01", "CAV-01", "RNG-01"]
const FIELD_LIMIT := 24

# Match representedMembers60, including the original server indices used by combat events.
static func field_slots(bag: Dictionary, roster: Array) -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var assigned: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var total := 0
	for id in bag:
		var count := int(bag[id])
		if count > 0:
			assigned.append({"id":id, "total":count, "count":1, "fraction":0.0, "order":assigned.size()})
			total += count
	if total > FIELD_LIMIT:
		var remaining := FIELD_LIMIT - assigned.size()
		var spare := total - assigned.size()
		for entry in assigned:
			var quota := float(remaining * (entry.total - 1)) / spare
			entry.count += floori(quota)
			entry.fraction = quota - floori(quota)
		remaining = FIELD_LIMIT
		for entry in assigned:
			remaining -= int(entry.count)
		var sorted := assigned.duplicate()
		sorted.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a.order < b.order if a.fraction == b.fraction else a.fraction > b.fraction)
		for entry in sorted:
			if remaining > 0 and entry.count < entry.total:
				entry.count += 1
				remaining -= 1
	else:
		for entry in assigned:
			entry.count = entry.total
	var roles := {}
	for unit in roster:
		roles[unit.id] = unit.training_building
	var groups := {"INF-01":[], "CAV-01":[], "RNG-01":[]}
	for entry in assigned:
		var role: String = roles.get(entry.id, "INF-01")
		for index in range(int(entry.count)):
			groups[role].append({"id":entry.id,"index":index})
	var slots: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var depth := 0.0
	for role in GROUPS:
		var members: Array = groups[role]
		var mounted: bool = role == "CAV-01"
		var width := 4 if mounted else 5
		var spacing := 2.4 if mounted else 1.45
		var row_depth := 3.7 if mounted else 1.7
		for i in range(members.size()):
			var row := floori(float(i) / width)
			var count := mini(width, members.size() - row * width)
			var member: Dictionary = members[i]
			member.side = (i % width - (count - 1) * 0.5) * spacing
			member.rear = depth + row * row_depth
			member.ranged = role == "RNG-01"
			member.mounted = mounted
			slots.append(member)
		if not members.is_empty():
			depth += ceilf(float(members.size()) / width) * row_depth + (0.8 if mounted else 0.45)
	return slots

static func parade_slots(bag: Dictionary, roster: Array) -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var groups := {"INF-01":[], "CAV-01":[], "RNG-01":[]}
	var remaining := bag.duplicate()
	for role in GROUPS:
		var members: Array = groups[role]
		var limit := 4 if role == "CAV-01" else 24
		while members.size() < limit:
			var size_before := members.size()
			for unit in roster:
				if unit.training_building == role and int(remaining.get(unit.id, 0)) > 0:
					members.append(unit.id)
					remaining[unit.id] = int(remaining[unit.id]) - 1
					if members.size() == limit:
						break
			if size_before == members.size():
				break
	var cavalry: Array = groups["CAV-01"]
	var foot_limit := 24 - ceili(cavalry.size() / 2.0)
	var slots: Array[Dictionary] = []
	for role in ["INF-01", "RNG-01"]:
		var members: Array = groups[role]
		var side := -1.0 if role == "INF-01" else 1.0
		for i in range(mini(foot_limit, members.size())):
			var x := side * (4.1 + floori(i / 12.0) * 4.5) + (i % 3 - 1) * 1.05
			slots.append({"id":members[i], "offset":Vector2(x, -6 + floori((i % 12) / 3.0) * 1.45), "mounted":false})
	for i in range(cavalry.size()):
		slots.append({"id":cavalry[i], "offset":Vector2(1.2 if i % 2 else -1.2, -5 + floori(i / 2.0) * 4), "mounted":true})
	return slots
````

````gdscript
var caption := ""
var symbol := "menu"
var glyph: Texture2D

func _ready() -> void:
	custom_minimum_size = Vector2(84, 88)
	size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_END
	size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
	for state in ["normal", "hover", "pressed", "focus", "disabled"]:
		add_theme_stylebox_override(state, StyleBoxEmpty.new())
	glyph = load("res://assets/ui/actions/" + symbol + ".svg")
	mouse_entered.connect(queue_redraw)
	mouse_exited.connect(queue_redraw)
	button_down.connect(queue_redraw)
	button_up.connect(queue_redraw)
	focus_entered.connect(queue_redraw)
	focus_exited.connect(queue_redraw)

func _draw() -> void:
	var center := Vector2(size.x * 0.5, 33)
	var pressed_now := is_pressed()
	var highlighted := is_hovered() or has_focus()
	if pressed_now:
		center.y += 2
	if glyph != null:
		draw_texture_rect(glyph, Rect2(center - Vector2(32,32),Vector2(64,64)),false)
	if highlighted:
		draw_arc(center, 29, 0, TAU, 64, Color("#ffe2a0"), 1.8, true)
	var font := get_theme_default_font()
	var baseline := Vector2((size.x-font.get_string_size(caption,HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT,-1,15).x)*0.5,80)
	draw_string_outline(font,baseline,caption,HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT,-1,15,5,Color("#111821"))
	draw_string(font,baseline,caption,HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT,-1,15,Color("#fff1d2"))
````
