````gdscript
			inspect_settlement(item)
			return

func inspect_world_plot(item: Dictionary) -> void:
	open_modal("Свободное место", "right", "ПЕРЕСЕЛЕНИЕ")
	var current: String = state.city.get("worldSlotId", "")
	if not current.begins_with("slot-") or not current.trim_prefix("slot-").is_valid_int():
		return
	var reason: String = state.world.get("relocationReason", "")
	if not reason.is_empty():
		row(modal_body, reason)
		return
	row(modal_body, "Координаты · %d : %d" % [roundi(float(item.x) + 150), roundi(float(item.z) + 120)])
	var order := {"kind":"relocateCity","slot":int(item.slot.trim_prefix("slot-")),"fromSlot":int(current.trim_prefix("slot-"))}
	button(modal_body, "Выбрать другое место", show_relocation)
	var occupied: bool = state.world.cities.any(func(city): return city.slot == item.slot)
	if occupied:
		row(modal_body, "Это место уже занято. Выбери другое.")
	panel_action(modal_body, "Подтвердить переселение", command.bind(order, focus_own_city),
		not player_id.is_empty() and not occupied)

func show_relocation() -> void:
	if map_name != "world" or scene_loading or player_id.is_empty():
		return
	close_modal()
	relocation_pins.show()
	relocation_pins.queue_redraw()
	message.text = "Выбери зелёную метку или открой список свободных мест"

func draw_relocation_pins() -> void:
	if map_name != "world" or not is_instance_valid(stage):
		return
	for marker in stage.empty_plots:
		if stage.camera.is_position_behind(marker.position):
			continue
		var point := stage.camera.unproject_position(marker.position)
		if get_viewport().get_visible_rect().has_point(point):
			relocation_pins.draw_circle(point, 13.0, Color("#174a37"))
			relocation_pins.draw_arc(point, 13.0, 0, TAU, 20, Color("#8cf5ad"), 2.0, true)
			relocation_pins.draw_line(point - Vector2(5, 0), point + Vector2(5, 0), Color.WHITE, 2)
			relocation_pins.draw_line(point - Vector2(0, 5), point + Vector2(0, 5), Color.WHITE, 2)

func show_relocation_list() -> void:
	open_modal("Переселение", "right")
	var origin := stage.target
	for city in state.world.cities:
		if city.id == player_id:
			origin = stage.point(city.position)
			break
	var sites: Array = stage.empty_plots.duplicate()
	sites.sort_custom(func(a, b):
		return origin.distance_squared_to(a.position) < origin.distance_squared_to(b.position))
	if sites.is_empty():
		row(modal_body, "Свободных мест пока нет.")
	for site in sites:
		button(modal_body, "%d : %d" % [roundi(site.position.x + 150), roundi(site.position.z + 120)], func():
			stage.target = site.position
			stage.zoom = 85.0
			inspect_world_plot(site.item))

func inspect_plot(plot: Dictionary) -> void:
	open_modal("Участок", "right")
	row(modal_body, "Координаты · %d : %d" % [roundi(plot.x), roundi(plot.z)])
	var kingdom: Variant = state.city.get("kingdom")
	if not kingdom is Dictionary:
		for definition in data.buildings:
			if definition.base != "ECO-05" and data.types.has(definition.id):
				button(modal_body, definition.ru, command.bind({"kind":"legacyBuilding", "action":"place",
					"type":definition.id, "plotId":plot.id}), not player_id.is_empty() and construction_order().is_empty())
		button(modal_body, "К списку участков", show_construction)
		return
	var builder_ready := construction_order().is_empty() and state.city.progress.get("job") == null
	if plot.id not in kingdom.get("clearedPlots", []) and not plot.id.begins_with("site-"):
		button(modal_body, "Расчистить · 15 еды · 6 сек.", command.bind({"kind":"kingdom","action":"clear","plotId":plot.id}),
			not player_id.is_empty() and builder_ready and balance("food") >= 15)
		button(modal_body, "К списку участков", show_construction)
		return
	var existing := {}
	for building in state.city.buildings:
		existing[building_definition(building.type).get("base", building.type)] = true
	var hall := hall_level()
	for definition in data.buildings:
		if definition.base == "ECO-05" or existing.has(definition.base) or not data.types.has(definition.id):
			continue
		var required := int(data.unlockLevels.get(definition.id, 3))
		if required <= hall:
			var terms: Dictionary = data.buildingTerms[definition.id]
			var order := {"kind":"kingdom","action":"build","plotId":plot.id,"type":definition.id}
			button(modal_body, "%s · %s · %d сек." % [definition.ru, cost_label(terms.cost), int(terms.seconds)], command.bind(order),
				not player_id.is_empty() and builder_ready and can_afford(terms.cost))
		else:
			row(modal_body, "%s · ратуша %d" % [definition.ru, required])
	button(modal_body, "К списку участков", show_construction)

func pick_tree(screen_position: Vector2) -> void:
	var distance := 18.0
	var selected := {}
	var felled: Array = state.city.get("progress", {}).get("felled", [])
	for tree in stage.selectable_trees:
		var p := stage.array_point(tree.p) + Vector3(0, 1, 0)
		if p.distance_squared_to(stage.camera_target) > 68.0 * 68.0 or stage.camera.is_position_behind(p):
			continue
		var gap := stage.camera.unproject_position(p).distance_to(screen_position)
		if gap < distance:
			distance = gap
			selected = tree
	if selected.is_empty():
		return
	open_modal(selected.name)
	if selected.id in felled:
		var order := {"kind":"resource","action":"site","id":selected.id}
		button(modal_body,"Подготовить участок",command.bind(order),not player_id.is_empty())
	else:
		row(modal_body, "%d дерева · %.0f сек." % [int(selected.wood), float(selected.durationMs) / 1000])
		var order := {"kind":"resource","action":"tree","id":selected.id}
		button(modal_body,"Срубить",command.bind(order),not player_id.is_empty())

func run_smoke() -> void:
	await get_tree().process_frame
	if stage.place_markers.size() != 4 or endpoint == "" or player_id == "":
		get_tree().quit(2)
		return
	var city_children: int = stage.trees.node.get_child_count()
	if stage.parade_slots.size() != int(state.healthy.get("ENG-09", 0)):
		printerr("Parade composition differs from the server garrison.")
		get_tree().quit(23)
		return
	if stage.terrain_bounds.size.x < 670.0 or stage.terrain_bounds.size.z < 670.0:
		get_tree().quit(14)
		return
	var barracks: Dictionary = state.city.buildings.filter(func(b): return b.type == "MIL-01")[0]
	inspect_building(barracks)
	var footman := unit_for_id("ENG-09")
	if footman.is_empty() or not modal.visible:
		get_tree().quit(10)
		return
	show_recruitment(barracks, footman, true)
	var preview_view := modal_body.find_child("RecruitPreview", true, false) as SubViewportContainer
	var preview_deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + 5000
	while preview_view != null and preview_view.get_child_count() == 0 and Time.get_ticks_msec() < preview_deadline:
		await get_tree().process_frame
	if preview_view == null or preview_view.get_child_count() != 1 or data.unitModels.size() != 10:
		get_tree().quit(15)
		return
	var training: Dictionary = await request("command", {"kind":"train", "buildingId":barracks.id, "unitId":footman.id, "quantity":2})
	var queue: Array = training.get("city", {}).get("military", {}).get("queues", {}).get(barracks.id, [])
	if queue.size() != 1 or int(queue[0].quantity) != 2:
		printerr(training.get("error", "Training order missing"))
		get_tree().quit(11)
		return
	accept(training)
	stage.present_training(state, float(queue[0].startsAt) + 1)
	var training_actor := stage.training.get_node_or_null(NodePath(barracks.id))
	if training_actor == null or training_actor.get_meta("clip", "") != data.unitModels[footman.id].drill:
		get_tree().quit(22)
		return
	var cancelled: Dictionary = await request("command", {"kind":"cancelTraining", "orderId":queue[0].id})
	if not cancelled.has("city") or not cancelled.city.military.queues.get(barracks.id, []).is_empty():
		get_tree().quit(12)
		return
	accept(cancelled)
	show_economy("wood")
	if modal_title.text != RESOURCE_NAMES.wood or modal_body.get_child_count() < RESOURCE_NAMES.size():
		get_tree().quit(32)
		return
	var food: int = balance("food")
	state.city.demo.wallet.food = capacity() + 1500
	show_economy("food")
	var full: Array = modal_body.find_children("*", "Label", true, false)
	if not full.any(func(label): return label.text == "Еда — " + format_balance(capacity() + 1500)) or \
		not full.any(func(label): return label.text.begins_with("Производство приостановлено")):
		get_tree().quit(40)
		return
	state.city.demo.wallet.food = mini(food, capacity() - 1)
	show_economy("food")
	if modal_body.find_children("*", "Label", true, false).any(func(label): return label.text.begins_with("Производство приостановлено")):
		get_tree().quit(41)
		return
	state.city.demo.wallet.food = food
	close_modal()
	var center := stage.target
	var original_zoom := stage.zoom
	var touch := InputEventScreenTouch.new()
	touch.index = 0
	touch.pressed = true
	touch.position = Vector2(540, 280)
	_unhandled_input(touch)
	var drag := InputEventScreenDrag.new()
	drag.index = 0
	drag.position = Vector2(570, 280)
	drag.relative = Vector2(30, 0)
	_input(drag)
	if stage.target == center:
		get_tree().quit(42)
		return
	touch.index = 1
	touch.position = Vector2(730, 280)
	_unhandled_input(touch)
	drag.index = 1
	drag.position = Vector2(780, 280)
	drag.relative = Vector2(50, 0)
	_input(drag)
	if stage.zoom >= original_zoom:
		get_tree().quit(43)
		return
	touch.pressed = false
	_input(touch)
	touch.index = 0
	_input(touch)
	if not fingers.is_empty():
		get_tree().quit(44)
		return
	stage.target = center
	stage.zoom = original_zoom
	stage.update_camera(0.0, true)
	show_construction()
	if stage.empty_plots.is_empty() or modal_body.get_child_count() < stage.empty_plots.size():
		get_tree().quit(33)
		return
	show_military_cultures()
	if modal_body.get_child_count() != data.militaryCultures.size() + 1:
		get_tree().quit(34)
		return
	if not await smoke_city_march_preview():
		get_tree().quit(31)
		return
	var city_stage := stage
	await show_scene("world")
	await get_tree().process_frame
	var world_children: int = stage.scenery.get_child_count()
	if city_children == 0 or world_children == 0 or stage.place_markers.is_empty() or not stage.has_node("Roads") or not stage.has_node("Water"):
		get_tree().quit(3)
		return
	var original_view := stage.view_state()
	rotate_world()
	stage.update_camera(0.0, true)

	get_tree().quit()

func smoke_city_march_preview() -> bool:
	var target: Dictionary = state.encounters.filter(func(item): return int(item.level) == 1)[0]
	inspect_encounter(target)
	var attack := modal_footer.get_child(0) as Button
	if attack.text != "Атаковать":
		return false
	attack.pressed.emit()
	var send := modal_footer.find_child("MarchSend", true, false) as Button
	var quote := modal_body.find_child("MarchQuote", true, false) as Label
	var quantity := modal_body.find_children("*", "SpinBox", true, false)[0] as SpinBox
	if quantity.value <= 0 or not send.disabled or modal_title.text != "Создать войско":
		return false
	var clear_button := modal_footer.get_children().filter(func(child): return child is Button and child.text == "Очистить")[0] as Button
	clear_button.pressed.emit()
	if not send.disabled or quote.text != "Выберите воинов":
		return false
	quantity.value = 1
	var deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + 8000
	var retry := modal_body.find_children("*", "Button", true, false).filter(func(child): return child.text == "Повторить расчёт")[0] as Button
	while send.disabled and not retry.visible and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:
		await get_tree().process_frame
	if OS.get_environment("DRENOVAR_TEST_CITY_PREVIEW_TIMEOUT") == "1":
		if not retry.visible or quantity.value != 1 or not send.disabled:
			return false
		retry.pressed.emit()
		deadline = Time.get_ticks_msec() + 8000
		while send.disabled and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:
			await get_tree().process_frame
		print("DRENOVAR native smoke: recovered from city march preview timeout.")
	var confirmed := not send.disabled and is_instance_valid(stage.route_preview) and quote.text.begins_with("Прибытие")
	if not confirmed:
		printerr("The city march did not calculate the route inline: ", quote.text)
	close_modal()
	return confirmed

func advance_test_clock(value: int) -> void:
	if not loopback or not smoke:
		return
	var client := HTTPRequest.new()
	add_child(client)
	client.timeout = 4.0
	if client.request(endpoint + "/test/clock?time=" + str(value), [], HTTPClient.METHOD_POST) == OK:
		await client.request_completed
	client.queue_free()
````

````gdscript
extends Control

var points := PackedVector2Array()

func set_path(path: Array) -> void:
	points.clear()
	for point in path:
		points.append(Vector2(point.x, point.z))
	visible = points.size() > 1
	queue_redraw()

func _draw() -> void:
	if points.size() < 2:
		return
	var bounds := Rect2(points[0], Vector2.ZERO)
	for point in points:
		bounds = bounds.expand(point)
	var inner_size := size - Vector2(22, 22)
	var scale_factor := minf(inner_size.x / maxf(bounds.size.x, 1), inner_size.y / maxf(bounds.size.y, 1))
	var line := PackedVector2Array()
	for point in points:
		line.append(size * 0.5 + (point - bounds.get_center()) * scale_factor)
	draw_style_box(get_theme_stylebox("normal", "LineEdit"), Rect2(Vector2.ZERO, size))
	draw_polyline(line, Color("#ebcc89"), 2.5, true)
	draw_circle(line[0], 4, Color("#ebcc89"), true, -1.0, true)
	draw_circle(line[-1], 6, Color("#d59978"), true, -1.0, true)
````

FILE 0025 / 0032
PATH: res://src/minimap.gd
ARCHIVE_PATH: DRENOVAR-iOS/project/src/minimap.gd
BYTES: 2170
SHA256: 2799d5c894d55b9ef5ea53431033685f574847da05ab6af3d9e9b20c2d64a739
ENDS_WITH_NEWLINE: true
````gdscript
extends Control

signal location_chosen(location: Vector2)

var terrain: ImageTexture
var map_layer := Control.new()
var cities: Array = []
var encounters: Array = []
var armies: Array = []
var player_id := ""
var focus := Vector2.ZERO

func setup(bytes: PackedByteArray) -> void:
	custom_minimum_size = Vector2(120, 96)
	terrain = ImageTexture.create_from_image(Image.create_from_data(260, 208, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8, bytes))
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	map_layer.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	map_layer.show_behind_parent = true
	add_child(map_layer)
	map_layer.draw.connect(draw_static_map)
	resized.connect(map_layer.queue_redraw)

func update_locations(next_cities: Array, next_encounters: Array, next_player: String) -> void:
	if cities == next_cities and encounters == next_encounters and player_id == next_player:
		return
	cities = next_cities
	encounters = next_encounters
	player_id = next_player
	map_layer.queue_redraw()

func on_map(p: Vector2) -> Vector2:
	return (p + Vector2(150, 120)) / Vector2(300, 240) * size

func draw_static_map() -> void:
	if terrain == null:
		return
	map_layer.draw_texture_rect(terrain, Rect2(Vector2.ZERO, size), false)
	for city in cities:
		var p := Vector2(city.position.x, city.position.z)
		map_layer.draw_circle(on_map(p), 2.4, Color("#f5d083") if city.id == player_id else Color("#dce9df"), true, -1, true)
	for encounter in encounters:
		if encounter.status != "defeated" and int(encounter.get("hp", 1)) > 0:
			map_layer.draw_circle(on_map(Vector2(encounter.position.x, encounter.position.z)), 1.6, Color("#e79979"))

func _draw() -> void:
	for army in armies:
		var p := Vector2(army.position.x, army.position.z)
		draw_circle(on_map(p), 2.0, Color("#bde6ff"))
	draw_arc(on_map(focus), 4.5, 0.0, TAU, 16, Color("#fff0c5"), 1.5, true)

func _gui_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	var pressed: bool = event is InputEventMouseButton and event.pressed and event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT
	pressed = pressed or (event is InputEventScreenTouch and event.pressed)
	if pressed:
		location_chosen.emit(event.position / size * Vector2(300, 240) - Vector2(150, 120))
		accept_event()
````

FILE 0026 / 0032
PATH: res://src/touch_scroll.gd
ARCHIVE_PATH: DRENOVAR-iOS/project/src/touch_scroll.gd
BYTES: 2935
SHA256: 20def4ea1ddcb1776a94a0f9482994578d37f803ed813f401045471f7ee78f72
ENDS_WITH_NEWLINE: true
````gdscript
extends ScrollContainer

const TAP_SLOP := 9.0
var active := false
var emulation_before_modal := true
var finger := -1
var origin := Vector2.ZERO
var previous := Vector2.ZERO
var finger_drag_started := false
var can_scroll := false
var offset := 0.0
var gesture_revision := 0
var slider: HSlider
var sliding := false

func set_modal_active(enabled: bool) -> void:
	cancel_touch()
	if active == enabled:
		return
	active = enabled
	if active:
		emulation_before_modal = Input.emulate_mouse_from_touch
		Input.emulate_mouse_from_touch = false
	else:
		Input.emulate_mouse_from_touch = emulation_before_modal

func cancel_touch() -> void:
	finger = -1
	slider = null
	sliding = false
	gesture_revision += 1

func _exit_tree() -> void:
	if active:
		Input.emulate_mouse_from_touch = emulation_before_modal

func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if not active or not is_visible_in_tree():
		return
	if event is InputEventScreenTouch:
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		if event.pressed and finger == -1:
			finger = event.index
			origin = event.position
			previous = origin
			offset = float(scroll_vertical)
			can_scroll = get_global_rect().has_point(origin)
			finger_drag_started = false
			slider = slider_at(origin) if can_scroll else null
			sliding = false
		elif not event.pressed and event.index == finger:
			finger = -1
			if not event.canceled and not finger_drag_started and event.position.distance_to(origin) < TAP_SLOP:
				tap.call_deferred(event.position, gesture_revision)
	elif event is InputEventScreenDrag:
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		if event.index != finger:
			return
		var travel: Vector2 = event.position - origin
		if not finger_drag_started and travel.length() >= TAP_SLOP:
			finger_drag_started = true
			sliding = is_instance_valid(slider) and absf(travel.x) > absf(travel.y)
		if sliding and is_instance_valid(slider):
			var track := slider.get_global_rect()
			slider.ratio = clampf((event.position.x - track.position.x) / track.size.x, 0.0, 1.0)
		elif can_scroll and finger_drag_started:
			var bar := get_v_scroll_bar()
			offset = clampf(offset + previous.y - event.position.y, 0.0, maxf(0.0, bar.max_value - bar.page))
			scroll_vertical = roundi(offset)
		previous = event.position

func slider_at(at: Vector2) -> HSlider:
	for item: HSlider in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("panel_slider"):
		if is_ancestor_of(item) and item.is_visible_in_tree() and item.editable and item.get_global_rect().has_point(at):
			return item
	return null

func tap(at: Vector2, revision: int) -> void:
	if not active or revision != gesture_revision or not is_visible_in_tree():
		return
	for is_pressed in [true, false]:
		var click := InputEventMouseButton.new()
		click.button_index = MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT
		click.pressed = is_pressed
		click.button_mask = MOUSE_BUTTON_MASK_LEFT if is_pressed else 0
		click.position = at
		click.global_position = at
		get_viewport().push_input(click, true)
````

````gdscript
extends RefCounted

const GOLD := Color("#e7c17d")
const INK := Color("#111916")
const TEXT := Color("#f6f0e4")
const MUTED := Color("#b9c5b7")
const STROKE := Color("#65573e")
const RESOURCES := {
	"food":Color("#e5c56e"), "wood":Color("#dc9a61"), "stone":Color("#c2ced3"),
	"iron":Color("#8fb4d2"), "coins":Color("#efbd5f")
}

static func style(fill: Color, stroke: Color, radius: int, horizontal: float, vertical: float) -> StyleBoxFlat:
	var box := StyleBoxFlat.new()
	box.bg_color = fill
	box.border_color = stroke
	box.set_border_width_all(1)
	box.set_corner_radius_all(radius)
	box.content_margin_left = horizontal
	box.content_margin_right = horizontal
	box.content_margin_top = vertical
	box.content_margin_bottom = vertical
	return box

static func panel_style(inset := 14, dialog := false) -> StyleBoxFlat:
	var box := style(Color("#19232fef") if dialog else Color("#151b22cf"), STROKE, 3, inset, inset)
	box.border_width_top = 2
	box.shadow_color = Color("#080c0ba6")
	box.shadow_size = 10 if dialog else 5
	box.shadow_offset = Vector2(0, 4)
	return box

static func button_styles(accent: Color = GOLD, filled := false) -> Dictionary:
	var base := Color("#273749").lerp(accent, 0.13 if filled else 0.035)
	var border := accent.darkened(0.43) if filled else Color("#7a8791")
	var focus := style(Color.TRANSPARENT, accent, 2, 0, 0)
	focus.draw_center = false
	focus.set_border_width_all(2)
	return {
		"normal":style(base, border, 2, 12, 6),
		"hover":style(base.lerp(accent, 0.23), accent.darkened(0.25), 2, 12, 6),
		"pressed":style(base.lerp(accent, 0.32), accent, 2, 12, 6),
		"disabled":style(Color("#242a30"), Color("#444e56"), 2, 12, 6),
		"focus":focus
	}

static func accent_button(control: Button, accent: Color = GOLD) -> void:
	var styles := button_styles(accent, true)
	for state in styles:
		control.add_theme_stylebox_override(state, styles[state])

static func hud_button(control: Button, framed := false) -> void:
	for state in ["normal", "hover", "pressed", "disabled", "focus"]:
		var fill := Color("#201c16b8") if framed else Color.TRANSPARENT
		if state == "hover":
			fill = Color("#d8b77322")
		elif state == "pressed":
			fill = Color("#d8b77344")
		var stroke := Color("#a68d5c99") if framed or state == "focus" else Color.TRANSPARENT
		var box := style(fill, stroke, 2, 10, 0)
		control.add_theme_stylebox_override(state, box)

static func make_theme() -> Theme:
	var theme := Theme.new()
	theme.default_font_size = 18
	theme.set_color("font_color", "Label", TEXT)
	for type in ["Button", "LineEdit", "CheckButton"]:
		theme.set_color("font_color", type, TEXT)
		theme.set_color("font_hover_color", type, Color.WHITE)
		theme.set_color("font_pressed_color", type, Color.WHITE)
		theme.set_color("font_focus_color", type, TEXT)
		theme.set_color("font_disabled_color", type, Color("#819086"))
	for type in ["Button", "OptionButton"]:
		var styles := button_styles()
		for state in styles:
			theme.set_stylebox(state, type, styles[state])
	theme.set_stylebox("normal", "LineEdit", style(Color("#0e1915"), Color("#607264"), 8, 10, 6))
	theme.set_stylebox("focus", "LineEdit", style(Color("#17251e"), GOLD, 8, 10, 6))
	theme.set_stylebox("panel", "PopupMenu", panel_style(10))
	theme.set_stylebox("hover", "PopupMenu", style(Color("#405447"), STROKE, 6, 8, 6))
	theme.set_stylebox("scroll", "VScrollBar", style(Color("#1a2821"), Color.TRANSPARENT, 4, 2, 2))
	for state in ["grabber", "grabber_highlight", "grabber_pressed"]:
		theme.set_stylebox(state, "VScrollBar", style(Color("#778675"), Color.TRANSPARENT, 4, 3, 3))
	return theme
````

````gdscript
extends RefCounted

static var contact_material: StandardMaterial3D

static func prepare(actor: Node3D) -> void:
	for part: MeshInstance3D in actor.find_children("*", "MeshInstance3D", true, false):
		if part.mesh == null:
			continue
		for surface in part.mesh.get_surface_count():
			var material := part.get_active_material(surface) as BaseMaterial3D
			if material == null or material.has_meta("drenovar_warrior_surface"):
				continue
			material.set_meta("drenovar_warrior_surface", true)
			material.texture_filter = BaseMaterial3D.TEXTURE_FILTER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS_ANISOTROPIC
			var name := material.resource_name.to_lower()
			if name.contains("eye"):
				material.metallic = 0.0
				material.metallic_specular = 0.44
			elif has_part(name, ["skin", "_head", "horsecoat", "horsehair", "beard"]):
				material.metallic_specular = 0.28
				material.roughness = maxf(material.roughness, 0.85)
				material.clearcoat_enabled = false
			elif has_part(name, ["cloth", "wool", "linen", "pants", "glove", "leather", "boot", "thread", "wood", "shieldpaint"]):
				material.metallic = 0.0
				material.metallic_specular = 0.32
				material.roughness = maxf(material.roughness, 0.9)
				material.clearcoat_enabled = false
			elif has_part(name, ["steel", "iron", "mail", "brass", "gold", "sword", "edge"]):
				material.metallic_specular = 0.52
				material.roughness = maxf(material.roughness, 0.63)

static func has_part(name: String, parts: Array[String]) -> bool:
	for part in parts:
		if part in name:
			return true
	return false

static func ground_shadow(parent: Node3D, bounds: AABB) -> void:
	if contact_material == null:
		var fade := Gradient.new()
		fade.colors = PackedColorArray([Color("#080c0ba1"), Color("#080c0b00")])
		var texture := GradientTexture2D.new()
		texture.gradient = fade
		texture.width = 64
		texture.height = 64
		texture.fill = GradientTexture2D.FILL_RADIAL
		texture.fill_from = Vector2(0.5, 0.5)
		texture.fill_to = Vector2(1.0, 0.5)
		contact_material = StandardMaterial3D.new()
		contact_material.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
		contact_material.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
		contact_material.albedo_texture = texture
	var plane := PlaneMesh.new()
	plane.size = Vector2(maxf(bounds.size.x * 0.9, 0.8), maxf(bounds.size.z * 1.05, 0.75))
	var shadow := MeshInstance3D.new()
	shadow.mesh = plane
	shadow.material_override = contact_material
	shadow.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	shadow.position = Vector3(bounds.get_center().x, bounds.position.y - 0.01, bounds.get_center().z)
	parent.add_child(shadow)
````

````glsl
shader_type spatial;
render_mode cull_disabled;

uniform vec4 water_color : source_color = vec4(0.19, 0.43, 0.46, 1.0);
varying vec2 water_position;

void vertex() {
	water_position = (MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(VERTEX, 1.0)).xz;
}

void fragment() {
	vec2 p = water_position;
	float broad = sin(dot(p, vec2(0.42, 0.23)) + TIME * 0.7);
	float fine = sin(dot(p, vec2(-0.61, 0.89)) - TIME * 1.15 + broad * 0.55);
	vec3 ripple = mat3(VIEW_MATRIX) * vec3(0.075 * broad, 0.0, 0.06 * fine);
	NORMAL = normalize(NORMAL + ripple);
	float edge = pow(1.0 - clamp(abs(dot(NORMAL, VIEW)), 0.0, 1.0), 3.0);
	ALBEDO = mix(water_color.rgb * (0.95 + fine * 0.04), vec3(0.22, 0.35, 0.37), edge * 0.36);
	ROUGHNESS = 0.29 + 0.04 * broad;
	SPECULAR = 0.52;
}
````

````gdscript
extends RefCounted

const Palette = preload("res://src/ui_theme.gd")
const BRASS := Color("#a4814d")
const IVORY := Color("#f2e7d0")
const GOLD := Color("#ebcc89")
static var gradients := {}

static func gradient_style(top: Color, bottom: Color, edge: Color, padding: int, radius := 7) -> StyleBoxTexture:
	var key := "%s:%s:%s:%d:%d" % [top.to_html(), bottom.to_html(), edge.to_html(), padding, radius]
	if gradients.has(key):
		return gradients[key]
	var size := maxi(32, radius * 2 + 4)
	var image := Image.create_empty(size, size, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	for y in size:
		var shade := top.lerp(bottom, float(y) / (size - 1))
		for x in size:
			var corner := Vector2(maxi(radius - mini(x, size - 1 - x), 0), maxi(radius - mini(y, size - 1 - y), 0))
			var distance := corner.length()
			if distance > radius:
				continue
			var border := distance > radius - 1.0
			image.set_pixel(x, y, edge if border else shade)
	var style := StyleBoxTexture.new()
	style.texture = ImageTexture.create_from_image(image)
	for side in [SIDE_LEFT, SIDE_TOP, SIDE_RIGHT, SIDE_BOTTOM]:
		style.set_texture_margin(side, radius + 1)
		style.set_content_margin(side, padding)
	gradients[key] = style
	return style

static func make_theme(base: Theme) -> Theme:
	var theme := base.duplicate() as Theme
	theme.default_font_size = 19
	theme.set_color("font_color", "Label", IVORY)
	var normal := Palette.style(Color("#272c31"), BRASS, 7, 12, 6)
	var hover := Palette.style(Color("#353d44"), GOLD, 7, 12, 6)
	var pressed := Palette.style(Color("#191d21"), BRASS, 7, 12, 6)
	var disabled := Palette.style(Color("#3b3e3c"), Color("#81715d"), 7, 12, 6)
	var focus := Palette.style(Color.TRANSPARENT, GOLD, 7, 0, 0)
	focus.draw_center = false
	focus.set_border_width_all(2)
	for type in ["Button", "OptionButton"]:
		theme.set_stylebox("normal", type, normal)
		theme.set_stylebox("hover", type, hover)
		theme.set_stylebox("pressed", type, pressed)
		theme.set_stylebox("disabled", type, disabled)
		theme.set_stylebox("focus", type, focus)
		theme.set_color("font_color", type, IVORY)
		theme.set_color("font_disabled_color", type, Color("#c2b7a3"))
	theme.set_stylebox("normal", "LineEdit", Palette.style(Color("#20252a"), BRASS, 5, 8, 4))
	theme.set_stylebox("scroll", "VScrollBar", Palette.style(Color("#272c31"), Color.TRANSPARENT, 3, 3, 2))
	for state in ["grabber", "grabber_highlight", "grabber_pressed"]:
		theme.set_stylebox(state, "VScrollBar", Palette.style(BRASS, Color.TRANSPARENT, 3, 3, 3))
	return theme

static func primary(control: Button, danger := false) -> void:
	var top := Color("#894d49") if danger else Color("#f1d497")
	var bottom := Color("#773c39") if danger else Color("#b98b4c")
	var edge := Color("#d59978") if danger else Color("#efce87")
	var text := Color("#fff0d9") if danger else Color("#221b13")
	control.add_theme_stylebox_override("normal", gradient_style(top, bottom, edge, 8))
	control.add_theme_stylebox_override("hover", gradient_style(top.lightened(0.1), bottom.lightened(0.1), edge, 8))
	control.add_theme_stylebox_override("pressed", gradient_style(top.darkened(0.12), bottom.darkened(0.12), edge, 8))
	for state in ["font_color", "font_hover_color", "font_focus_color", "font_pressed_color"]:
		control.add_theme_color_override(state, text)
````

````gdscript
func setup(game_data: Dictionary, state: Dictionary, scene_name: String, saved_view: Dictionary) -> void:
	data = game_data
	map_name = scene_name
	name = scene_name.capitalize()

	env.ambient_light_energy = 0.58 if is_city else 0.65
	env.tonemap_mode = Environment.TONE_MAPPER_FILMIC

	env.fog_light_color = env.background_color

	var sun := DirectionalLight3D.new()
	sun.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-48, -32, 0)
	shadow_direction = -sun.basis.z
	sun.light_color = Color("#ffe4b8") if is_city else Color("#ffefd0")
	sun.light_energy = 1.85
	sun.shadow_enabled = true
	sun.directional_shadow_max_distance = 150 if is_city else 260
	if is_city:
		# One atlas projection covers the orthographic city without repeating casters across splits.
		sun.directional_shadow_mode = DirectionalLight3D.SHADOW_ORTHOGONAL
	add_child(sun)
	var ground_material := ShaderMaterial.new()
	ground_material.shader = BIOME_SHADER
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("settlement", is_city)
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("grass", load(SURFACES + "grass20-albedo.webp"))
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("earth", load(SURFACES + "soil-albedo.webp"))
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("rock", load(data.stoneTexture))
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("normal_texture", load(SURFACES + "grass20-normal.png"))
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("roughness_texture", load(SURFACES + "grass20-roughness.png"))
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("soil_normal", load(SURFACES + "soil-normal.png"))
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("soil_roughness", load(SURFACES + "soil-roughness.png"))
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("stone_normal", load(SURFACES + "stone-normal.png"))
	ground_material.set_shader_parameter("stone_roughness", load(SURFACES + "stone-roughness.png"))
	if is_city:
		meadow_material = ShaderMaterial.new()
		meadow_material.shader = MEADOW_SHADER
		meadow_stone = StandardMaterial3D.new()
		meadow_stone.albedo_texture = load(data.stoneTexture)
		meadow_stone.vertex_color_use_as_albedo = true
		meadow_stone.roughness = 0.94
	scenery_material = StandardMaterial3D.new()
	scenery_material.albedo_texture = load(data.environmentTexture)
	scenery_material.vertex_color_use_as_albedo = true
	scenery_material.cull_mode = BaseMaterial3D.CULL_DISABLED
	scenery_material.roughness = 0.95
	scenery_material.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA_SCISSOR
	scenery_material.alpha_scissor_threshold = 0.36

	var ground_paths: Array = data.cityGround if is_city else data.worldGround
	if not is_city:
		world_heights = Marshalls.base64_to_raw(data.worldHeights).to_float32_array()
	for path in ground_paths:
		var ground := scene_resource(path).instantiate()
		add_child(ground)
		for mesh in ground.find_children("*", "MeshInstance3D", true, false):
			mesh.material_override = ground_material
			var bounds: AABB = mesh.transform * mesh.get_aabb()
			terrain_bounds = bounds if not terrain_bounds.has_volume() else terrain_bounds.merge(bounds)
	add_child(buildings)
	add_child(actors)
	add_child(creatures)
	add_child(training)
	add_child(scenery)
	add_child(city_ground_detail)
	add_child(parade)
	add_child(workers)
	add_child(effects)

	camera.projection = Camera3D.PROJECTION_ORTHOGONAL if is_city else Camera3D.PROJECTION_PERSPECTIVE
	camera.fov = 43.0
	camera.near = 0.5
	camera.far = 700.0 if is_city else 1100.0
	await get_tree().process_frame
	if is_city:
		zoom = 70.0
		target = Vector3(5, 0, -4)
		build_water()
	else:
		fit_world()
		await build_world_scenery()
		await get_tree().process_frame
		build_towns()
		build_world_water()

	if not saved_view.is_empty():

		world_yaw = saved_view.get("yaw", 0.0)
````
